# GPU portability of the OpenFOAM model set

각 구성요소를 **완전히 device에 상주하는 시간 루프** 기준으로 분류했습니다. 대상 하드웨어는 NVIDIA RTX 5070 Ti (Blackwell, sm_120, 16 GB), CUDA 13.3.

The bar is not "can a GPU help here" - almost anything can be accelerated somehow. The bar is: **can this run for the whole time loop without a host round-trip?** That is what decides whether a component can join a device-resident solver or whether it forces a synchronisation every step.

## Tiers

| Tier | Meaning |
|---|---|
| **A-trivial** | Elementwise or face-stencil work. Pure CUDA kernels, no library needed, fully device resident today. |
| **B-sparse-solve** | Cost is dominated by solving a sparse system. AMGX (multigrid) or cuDSS (direct) replaces the OpenFOAM solver; the assembly around it is tier A. |
| **C-fft** | Spectral or convolution work - cuFFT. |
| **D-hard** | Irregular, serial, topology-mutating or search-heavy. A device-resident port is a research project, not a transcription. |
| **E-cpu-only** | I/O, dictionaries, runtime selection, decomposition setup. Belongs on the host and should stay there. |

## Census

| Subsystem | A-trivial | B-sparse-solve | C-fft | D-hard | E-cpu-only | other | total |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Linear algebra: lduMatrix solvers, preconditioners, smoothers, AMG | 16 | 27 | 0 | 6 | 42 | 0 | 91 |
| Momentum transport / turbulence | 62 | 40 | 0 | 4 | 23 | 0 | 129 |
| Thermophysical and chemistry | 156 | 1 | 0 | 24 | 31 | 0 | 212 |
| Multiphase, two-phase, lagrangian, waves | 118 | 1 | 7 | 69 | 94 | 0 | 289 |
| finiteVolume discretisation schemes, surface interpolation and fvMatrix | 251 | 3 | 0 | 19 | 45 | 0 | 318 |
| Mesh: Mesh motion, run-time topology change, decomposition | 115 | 6 | 0 | 18 | 48 | 0 | 187 |
| Mesh: Mesh generation and manipulation | 33 | 0 | 0 | 28 | 84 | 0 | 145 |
| Mesh: Mesh tools and searching | 32 | 0 | 0 | 25 | 130 | 0 | 187 |
| Mesh: Core mesh data structures | 70 | 3 | 0 | 18 | 113 | 0 | 204 |
| **Total** | **853** | **81** | **7** | **211** | **610** | **0** | **1762** |

> **Why this total is 1762 and `01-model-catalog.md`'s is 1761.** The two
> documents catalogue the same OpenFOAM-12 component set and agree row for row
> in eight of the nine subsystems. The ninth, finiteVolume, carries one extra
> entry here: `extendedCellToFaceStencil::weightedSum`, the shared runtime
> kernel behind all the fit schemes. It is not runtime-selectable, so it is not
> a component in `01`'s sense and `01` does not list it; it *is* a distinct unit
> of porting work, which is what this document counts, so it is listed here.
> The difference is deliberate and is the whole of the discrepancy — verified by
> diffing the two finiteVolume tables entry by entry.
>
> Neither table can currently be regenerated: `_build_catalog.py` takes the
> `journal.jsonl` files the cataloguing pass produced, and those are not
> committed. Both tables are internally consistent (every subsystem count
> equals its own rows, and the totals equal the sums), which is what can be
> checked from the repository as it stands.

## Recommended porting order

속도 향상 대비 노력이 큰 순서입니다.

1. **Mesh + LDU addressing on the device** (tier A). 모든 것의 전제조건입니다. `lduAddressing`의 `lowerAddr`/`upperAddr`, `V`, `Sf`, `magSf`, `deltaCoeffs`, `weights`, `nonOrthCorrectionVectors`를 한 번 올리고 끝냅니다. 이미 `gpu/common/src/ofgpu_mesh.cu`에 있습니다. *(경로 주석: 이 문서가 쓰인 뒤 `gpu/` 트리 전체가 저장소에서 제거되었습니다 — `LICENSING.md`의 "What phase 0 actually removed" 참고. 지금 이 역할을 하는 파일은 `rust/src/mesh.rs`·`rust/src/mesh/`와 `rust/cuda/`의 커널들입니다.)*
2. **`fvm::` / `fvc::` Gauss operators** (tier A). 면 루프 하나가 커널 하나입니다. OpenFOAM의 scatter를 cell->face CSR gather로 뒤집는 것이 핵심입니다.
3. **The linear solver** (tier B). 난류 모델은 반복당 두 번의 sparse solve로 시간을 씁니다. 자체 PBiCGStab로도 충분하고, 압력 방정식이 들어오면 AMGX가 필요합니다.
4. **Eddy-viscosity RAS models** (tier A + B). k-epsilon, k-omega, kOmegaSST, realizableKE, RNGkEpsilon, SpalartAllmaras - 전부 같은 골격입니다. k-epsilon과 k-omega는 `gpu/kEpsilon`, `gpu/kOmega`에 구현되어 있습니다.
5. **Wall functions** (tier A). 벽면당 커널 하나. 벽셀 평균만 atomic 없이 gather로 바꾸면 됩니다.
6. **The SIMPLE/PIMPLE pressure equation** (tier B). 대칭 행렬이라 AMGX의 classical AMG가 가장 잘 맞습니다. `GAMG` + `DIC`를 대체합니다.
7. **LES SGS models** (tier A). 필터 연산이 face 스텐실이라 RAS와 같습니다. `dynamicLagrangian`만 시간 평균 필드가 추가로 필요합니다.
8. **Thermophysical property evaluation** (tier A). JANAF/NASA 다항식은 셀당 독립이라 이상적인 커널입니다.
9. **Chemistry ODE integration** (tier A-ish, but stiff). 셀당 독립이라 병렬성은 완벽하지만, 셀마다 스텝 수가 달라 warp divergence가 심합니다. 셀을 강성도로 정렬하거나 배치 solver를 쓰는 것이 정석입니다.
10. **Lagrangian particle tracking** (tier D). 셀 탐색, 동적 리스트, 충돌 - GPU 재설계가 필요합니다.
    *(후속 주석: `SPEC-LIT.md` §66이 이 판정의 셋 중 둘을 뒤집었습니다. **셀 탐색은 필요 없습니다** — 모든 파셀은 알려진 셀에서 태어나고 sub-step CFL 제한이 면 통과를 한 칸으로 묶으므로, 위치 갱신은 이미 존재하는 셀→면 CSR 위의 gather 워크입니다. **동적 리스트도 CUDA Graph를 깨지 않습니다** — 고정 기하 grid-stride 커널이 device 상주 `nActive`를 커널 안에서 읽으므로 graph는 한 번 캡처되고 갱신이 필요 없습니다. 남은 tier D는 **충돌** 그리고 셀당 침적(deposition)을 gather 형태로 만드는 `(cell, uid)` 정렬입니다. §66은 단방향 결합·비증발이며, 나머지는 이름을 들어 거부됩니다.)*
    *(추가 주석: 그 정렬을 **§67이 구현했습니다.** `(cell, uid)` 전순서 키에 대한 LSD 기수 정렬, 장치 배타 스캔, 셀별 파셀 CSR, 그리고 셀당 한 스레드 게더 — f64 원자연산 없이, 셀→면 CSR과 같은 모양으로. 정렬이 곧 정준화(canonicaliser)이므로 슬롯 순서·분사 순서·블록 수는 결과에 남지 않습니다. 비용은 §67.9에 측정치로 적혀 있습니다: 100만 슬롯에서 정렬+CSR 0.71 ms, 게더 0.52 ms. **이 판정에서 아직 유효하게 남은 것은 충돌 하나, 그리고 다면체 셀에서의 워크(§66.6)뿐입니다.**)*
    *(세 번째 주석: **§68이 양방향 결합을 붙였습니다.** 파셀 적분기가 실제로 가한 항력 임펄스 `m_eff(Δu_p − a_g h)`를 §18 소스 레지스트리를 통해 기체에 그대로 돌려주므로, 두 상 사이의 운동량 보존은 허용 오차가 아니라 항등식입니다(측정 8.3e-16). 현열도 같은 구조이며(측정 3.1e-16), 액적 온도가 유한한 열용량을 갖도록 `physics heating`이 추가되었습니다. 파셀이 하나도 없으면 기존 답은 비트 단위로 그대로입니다 — 등록된 것이 없으면 커널이 실행되지 않기 때문입니다. 게이트 68-C(Theobald 1981 소방 노즐 90건)는 **빗나가며**, 그 이유와 함께 `ofgpu-validate` 화면에 그대로 적힙니다: 정지 공기에서는 측정 도달거리의 61 %, 진공 상한은 199 %이므로 도달거리의 대부분은 **유입 공기**가 결정합니다. 이 주석이 쓰인 뒤 그 거부 목록은 두 번 줄었습니다: **§76/§77이 증발과 그 결합을**, **§78이 벽 충돌 영역 지도(Bai-Gosman)와 벽에 남는 질량의 원장을** 지었습니다. 여전히 이름을 들어 거부되는 것은 액적 복사 흡수(§68.13), 스플래시 자식 액적 생성(§78.7), 그리고 막(film) 수송(§78.11)입니다.)*
11. **Mesh generation and topology change** (tier D/E). `snappyHexMesh`, `polyTopoChange`, AMR은 host에 두는 것이 맞습니다.

## Mesh data structures on the GPU

한 번 올리면 정적 메쉬에서는 다시 건드리지 않는 배열입니다.

| OpenFOAM | Size | Why the GPU needs it |
|---|---|---|
| `lduAddr().lowerAddr()` (= `owner`) | nInternalFaces | 행렬 그래프의 절반 |
| `lduAddr().upperAddr()` (= `neighbour`) | nInternalFaces | 나머지 절반 |
| `mesh.V()` | nCells | 모든 체적 적분 |
| `mesh.C()` | nCells | 기울기, 비직교 보정 |
| `mesh.Sf()`, `mesh.magSf()` | nInternalFaces + nBoundaryFaces | 모든 면 적분 |
| `mesh.Cf()` | 면 전체 | 보간 가중치 |
| `surfaceInterpolation::weights()` | nInternalFaces | 선형 보간 |
| `nonOrthDeltaCoeffs()` | 면 전체 | laplacian, snGrad |
| `nonOrthCorrectionVectors()` | nInternalFaces | 비직교 명시적 보정 |
| patch `faceCells()` | 패치별 | 경계 조립 |
| `nearWallDist` / `y` | 벽 패치 | 벽함수 |

여기에 더해, OpenFOAM에는 없지만 GPU에는 **반드시 필요한** 것이 하나 있습니다: **cell -> face CSR**. OpenFOAM은 대각 성분을 `diag[owner[f]] -= ...` 처럼 면 루프에서 흩뿌립니다. GPU에서 그러려면 double atomic이 필요하고 합산 순서가 비결정적이 됩니다. 역맵을 만들어 두면 셀 하나가 스레드 하나로 자기 면들을 모으기만 하면 되고, 결과가 비트 단위로 재현됩니다.

**절대 device에 상주할 수 없는 메쉬 연산:**

- `polyTopoChange` 계열 (셀 분할/병합, layer addition, sliding interface) - 주소 배열 자체를 다시 만듭니다
- `snappyHexMesh`, `blockMesh` - 생성 단계이고 트리 탐색과 동적 자료구조를 씁니다
- `decomposePar` / `renumberMesh` - 그래프 분할 (METIS/Scotch)
- `meshSearch`, `treeBoundBox` 계열 - 재귀적 트리 탐색
- `fvMeshDistributor` - MPI 재분배

움직이지만 위상은 안 바뀌는 메쉬(`fvMeshMovers`, `motionSolvers`)는 중간입니다: 점 이동은 tier A, 기하 재계산도 tier A, 그런데 `displacementLaplacian` 같은 모션 솔버는 자체 sparse solve가 있으므로 tier B입니다.

## Library fit

### AMGX

대체 대상: `GAMG` (`src/OpenFOAM/matrices/lduMatrix/solvers/GAMG`)와 그 agglomeration (`pairGAMGAgglomeration`, `algebraicPairGAMGAgglomeration`, `faceAreaPairGAMGAgglomeration`), 그리고 `PCG`/`PBiCG`/`PBiCGStab` + `DIC`/`DILU`/`FDIC` 조합.

압력 방정식이 진짜 표적입니다. 비압축성 솔버 실행시간의 60~80 %가 여기 있고, 대칭 M-행렬이라 classical AMG가 가장 잘 듣습니다. AMGX는 setup과 solve가 모두 device에 있어서 host 왕복이 없습니다. LDU를 CSR로 한 번 순열해 두면 값 채우기는 gather 한 번입니다 (`GpuCsrMatrix`가 그렇게 되어 있습니다).

### cuDSS

대체 대상: 직접법이 필요한 작고 조밀한 계 - `simpleMatrix`, `scalarMatrices` (`LUDecompose`/`LUsolve`), 화학 반응 Jacobian, `EulerImplicit`/`ode` chemistry solver 내부의 dense solve.

메인 압력/속도 계에는 맞지 않습니다 - 3-D FV 행렬은 fill-in이 심해서 직접 분해가 반복법보다 훨씬 비쌉니다. 반면 화학은 종 개수만큼(보통 10~100) 작은 dense 계를 셀마다 풀어야 해서 배치 직접법이 정확히 맞습니다.

### cuFFT

대체 대상: `src/randomProcesses` (`fft`, `Kmesh`, `UOprocess`, `noiseModels`), `turbulentDFSEMInlet` / 합성 난류 유입 생성, 그리고 균일 격자 위의 스펙트럼 후처리 (`energySpectrum`).

범위가 좁습니다. OpenFOAM의 핵심 반복 루프에는 FFT가 없습니다 - 비정렬 격자 FV 코드이기 때문입니다. cuFFT가 값을 하는 곳은 LES 유입 조건 생성과 음향 후처리입니다.

### cuSPARSE / cuBLAS / CUB

직접적인 OpenFOAM 대응물은 없지만 자체 솔버의 재료입니다. cuSPARSE는 CSR SpMV, cuBLAS는 dot/axpy, CUB는 리덕션입니다. 이 프로젝트는 LDU gather SpMV를 직접 쓰고 리덕션만 CUB에 맡깁니다 - LDU 구조가 이미 최적이라 CSR로 바꿀 이유가 없기 때문입니다.

---

## Linear algebra: lduMatrix solvers, preconditioners, smoothers, AMG

### A-trivial  <sub>(16)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `CMULES` | custom-kernels | The correction machinery itself is the same bounded face/cell limiter arithmetic as MULES, applied on top of an already-solved implicit upwind field - so the code in this file is A-tier, fully parallel, fixed trip count. | Same scatter-add races and sign branching as MULES. The accompanying Euler-implicit upwind solve is a separate B-tier sparse solve done through fvMatrix, so the end-to-end alpha step is only as device-resident as that solver is. MULESCorr/nLimiterIter/alphaApplyPrevCorr are dictionary lookups in the alpha solver dict read inside the time loop - hoist them. applyPrevCorr keeps a stored correction field between steps, which must stay resident on device rather than being re-uploaded. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/finiteVolume/fvMatrices/solvers/MULES/CMULES.C` |
| `DiagonalPreconditioner<Type, DType, LUType>` | custom-kernels | w = inv(D) & r, one elementwise pass with a per-cell DType inverse held in registers. Fully parallel, no dependencies. | Files MISSING on this checkout (case collision with the scalar diagonalPreconditioner) - restore from upstream. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Preconditioners/DiagonalPreconditioner/ (DiagonalPreconditioner.H/.C)` |
| `DiagonalSolver<Type, DType, LUType>` | custom-kernels | psi = inv(D) & b componentwise over nCells. One elementwise kernel; for tensor DType the per-cell inv() is a fixed 3x3 inverse done in registers. No iteration, no reduction. | None algorithmically. Files DiagonalSolver.H/.C are MISSING on this checkout due to the case collision with the scalar diagonalSolver - restore from upstream. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Solvers/DiagonalSolver/ (DiagonalSolver.H/.C)` |
| `GAMGAgglomeration` | Thrust/CUB | The two operators that run inside the time loop are ideal GPU work: restrictField is a segmented scatter-add c^H[restrictAddressing[i]] += c^h[i] (atomicAdd or a sort-free CUB segmented reduce given the addressing is already grouped), and prolongField is a pure gather c^h[i] = c^H[restrictAddressing[i]]. Both are fully parallel over nCells with no dependencies. | The hierarchy CONSTRUCTION (agglomerateLduAddressing, the level lists, faceFlipMap) is host-only and irregular - see pairGAMGAgglomeration. Mitigated by the fact that this is a DemandDrivenMeshObject cached in the mesh registry (confirmed store()/foundObject() at lines 294-380), so on a static mesh it runs ONCE at setup and only the restrictAddressing arrays need uploading. On a moving or adaptive mesh it re-runs and becomes a hard host round-trip. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Solvers/GAMG/GAMGAgglomerations/GAMGAgglomeration/GAMGAgglomeration.C` |
| `GAMGInterface` | Thrust/CUB | The runtime operator is agglomerateCoeffs, a scatter-add coarseCoeffs[faceRestrictAddressing_[ffi]] += fineCoeffs[ffi] over the interface faces - an atomicAdd kernel or CUB segmented reduce. interfaceInternalField is a gather through faceCells_. Both fully parallel over patch faces, which are few compared to nCells. | Construction and the Istream reconstruction path (used when a coarse interface is rebuilt on a receiving rank after processor agglomeration) are host-only; on a single GPU that path is never taken. The debug branch calls max(faceRestrictAddressing_) which is a host reduction - compiled out unless debug is on. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Solvers/GAMG/interfaces/GAMGInterface/GAMGInterface.C` |
| `GAMGInterfaceField` | custom-kernels | updateInterfaceMatrix is result[faceCells[i]] -= coeffs[i]*psiNeighbour[i] - a gather, a multiply and a scatter-subtract over patch faces. Fully parallel, tiny relative to the interior face loop. | It is an abstract base reached by VIRTUAL DISPATCH from inside Amul's interface loop, once per patch per matrix-vector product. On device that dispatch must be flattened into a precomputed table of (faceCells, coeffs, transform) descriptors so the kernel is launched without touching the C++ vtable. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Solvers/GAMG/interfaceFields/GAMGInterfaceField/GAMGInterfaceField.C` |
| `LduMatrix<Type, DType, LUType>` | custom-kernels | Same LDU storage with DType diagonal and LUType off-diagonal coefficients. Field<DType>/Field<LUType> are contiguous VectorSpace arrays, so a device mirror is a straight SoA/AoS upload. The only added work over lduMatrix is that kernels must be instantiated per coefficient type. | Five instantiations (scalar, vector, sphericalTensor, symmTensor, tensor) from lduMatrices.C multiply the kernel count; DType operations need device-side inv()/dot() for tensors. On this checkout LduMatrix.H/.C/LduMatrixTemplates.C are MISSING due to the lduMatrix/LduMatrix case collision - they must be restored from upstream before anything here can be ported or even built. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/ (LduMatrix.H, LduMatrixI.H, lduMatrixTemplates.C, lduMatrices.C)` |
| `MULES` | custom-kernels | The best non-solver GPU target in this subsystem. The limiter is a FIXED nLimiterIter sweeps (default 3, no convergence test) of: a face loop accumulating sumlPhip/mSumlPhim into owner and neighbour by the sign of lambda*phiCorr, a cell loop doing max(min(...,1),0), and a face loop taking min() against the cell limiters. All bounded, all parallel, no data-dependent trip count. The extrema and psiMaxn/psiMinn setup are pure elementwise volScalarField algebra. | The face loops scatter-add into owner and neighbour, so they need atomicAdd or a CSR-style gather - and the sign branch (if lambdaPhiCorrf > 0) causes warp divergence, though it is cheap. MULEScontrols.lookupOrDefault<label>("nLimiterIter", 3) at line 213 is a dictionary HashTable lookup executed inside the limiter call, i.e. every time step and every alpha sub-cycle - hoist it. Boundary patch loops need flattening into one fused kernel. Parallel runs need a reduce for the extrema; serial does not. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/finiteVolume/fvMatrices/solvers/MULES/MULES.C` |
| `NoPreconditioner<Type, DType, LUType>` | custom-kernels | w = r for a Field<Type>. Device copy or buffer aliasing. | Files MISSING on this checkout (case collision with the scalar noPreconditioner) - restore from upstream before building. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Preconditioners/NoPreconditioner/ (NoPreconditioner.H/.C)` |
| `cyclicGAMGInterface` | custom-kernels | The one coarse-level interface that genuinely matters on a single GPU, because periodic boundaries are common and involve no MPI. The work is a gather from the neighbour patch's faceCells plus an optional rank-dependent tensor transform - a small fully parallel kernel over patch faces. | Construction is host-side (RTS lookup on the patch type word, plus the Istream constructor for the parallel path). The transform tensor must be uploaded once. Otherwise clean. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Solvers/GAMG/interfaces/cyclicGAMGInterface/cyclicGAMGInterface.C` |
| `cyclicGAMGInterfaceField` | custom-kernels | pnf = psiInternal[nbrFaceCells]; apply transform if doTransform; result[faceCells] -= coeffs*pnf. Three fully parallel steps over patch faces with no communication - this is the coarse-level interface field that actually runs on a single-GPU periodic case. | None at runtime. Construction goes through two RTS tables keyed on the patch type word (host, setup-only). The transform is a fixed tensor uploaded once. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Solvers/GAMG/interfaceFields/cyclicGAMGInterfaceField/cyclicGAMGInterfaceField.C` |
| `diagonalPreconditioner` | custom-kernels | w = rD*r, one elementwise multiply over nCells, with rD = 1/diag computed once in a second elementwise loop. Zero dependencies, perfectly coalesced, memory-bandwidth-bound. This is the preconditioner to fall back to when you want a fully device-resident Krylov loop with no triangular solve. | None. Converges far more slowly than DIC/DILU, so the win is per-iteration cost, not iteration count - measure the tradeoff. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Preconditioners/DiagonalPreconditioner/diagonalPreconditioner.C` |
| `diagonalSolver` | custom-kernels | The whole solve is one line: psi = source/matrix_.diag(). A single elementwise divide over nCells, no iteration, no reduction, no communication. Returns a hardcoded converged solverPerformance. | None. Note lduMatrix::solver::New short-circuits to this whenever matrix.diagonal() is true regardless of the solver keyword, so the device path must replicate that test on the matrix descriptor. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Solvers/DiagonalSolver/diagonalSolver.C` |
| `lduMatrix` | custom-kernels | The container is literally three contiguous scalarFields (diag_ nCells, upper_/lower_ nFaces) plus a reference to lduAddressing. Nothing here is algorithmic - it is exactly the LDU/COO layout you would upload to the device once and keep resident. diagonal()/symmetric()/asymmetric() are pointer-null tests on lowerPtr_/upperPtr_, resolvable at upload time into a device-side matrix descriptor. | lowerPtr_/upperPtr_ are demand-driven raw new'd pointers; lower() lazily allocates a copy of upper() for symmetric matrices, so the device mirror must decide sym/asym at build time rather than lazily. No host round-trip in the data itself. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/LduMatrix/lduMatrix.H` |
| `lduMatrix ATmul / Amul / Tmul / H / faceH / residual / sumA` | cuSPARSE | The hottest code in the subsystem and the clearest port target. Amul is one elementwise diagonal pass over nCells then a single face loop doing Apsi[uPtr[f]] += lower[f]*psi[lPtr[f]] and Apsi[lPtr[f]] += upper[f]*psi[uPtr[f]]. Tmul swaps upper/lower, residual negates and adds the source, sumA drops psi, H1 keeps only the off-diagonals - all the same face-scatter shape. Either write a CSR SpMV (ownerStartAddr already gives the row pointers) or keep the LDU form with atomicAdd. | The face loop is a scatter with write conflicts - two faces sharing a cell race - so it needs atomicAdd, a CSR gather formulation, or face colouring. atomicAdd on doubles gives non-deterministic summation order, so results will not be bit-reproducible run to run unless you use CSR gather (deterministic, and faster - prefer it). initMatrixInterfaces/updateMatrixInterfaces bracket every one of these loops with virtual dispatch and, in parallel, MPI - flatten the interface contributions into the same kernel. residual() also allocates a whole FieldField of negated boundary coefficients on every call, which should be a sign flag instead. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/LduMatrix/lduMatrixATmul.C` |
| `noPreconditioner` | custom-kernels | w = r. A device-to-device copy, or better, elided entirely by aliasing the buffers in the solver. Nothing to port beyond deleting the call. | None. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Preconditioners/NoPreconditioner/noPreconditioner.C` |

### B-sparse-solve  <sub>(27)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `DICGaussSeidelSmoother` | AMGX | Pure composition - holds a DICSmoother and a GaussSeidelSmoother and calls smooth() on each in turn. Inherits both serial chains back to back, so it is the worst of the two for latency, though it is the most robust GAMG smoother on the CPU. | Two independent sequential passes per sweep: DIC's level-scheduled substitution then GS's full serial cell chain. Both need different GPU replacements (SpSV vs multicolour), so the composition has to be rebuilt rather than ported. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Smoothers/DICGaussSeidel/DICGaussSeidelSmoother.C` |
| `DICPreconditioner` | cuSPARSE | precondition() is two strictly sequential face sweeps: the forward loop does wA[uPtr[face]] -= rD[u]*upper[face]*wA[lPtr[face]] in ascending face order, so wA at a cell is read only after every lower-numbered contributing face has written it - a textbook loop-carried dependency. This is an ILU(0)-style triangular solve and the GPU answer is cusparseSpSV with level scheduling, not a naive kernel. | Both sweeps are sequential; parallelism is limited to the level-set width, which on an unstructured mesh is roughly O(N^(2/3)) at best and often far worse. calcReciprocalD is ALSO sequential (rD[u] -= U*U/rD[l] with loop-carried rD) and it runs in the CONSTRUCTOR, which executes on every solve because PCG builds the preconditioner inside solve(). Level-scheduled execution reproduces the result exactly (dependency-respecting reorder), so numerics are safe - only concurrency is the issue. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Preconditioners/DICPreconditioner/DICPreconditioner.C` |
| `DICSmoother` | cuSPARSE | Per sweep: rA = b - A.psi (A-tier face loop), DIC forward/backward substitution on rA (the sequential part), psi += rA (elementwise). Two thirds of the work is trivially parallel; the substitution is the same level-scheduled triangular solve as DICPreconditioner. | The DIC substitution's loop-carried dependency, with concurrency capped by the level-set width. rD_ is computed once in the constructor by the sequential calcReciprocalD, but unlike the preconditioner the smoother object is built once per GAMGSolver construction rather than per Krylov iteration. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Smoothers/DIC/DICSmoother.C` |
| `DILUGaussSeidelSmoother` | AMGX | Composition of DILUSmoother then GaussSeidelSmoother, exactly parallel to DICGaussSeidel on the asymmetric side. Two serial passes chained. | Both constituent serial chains, each needing a different GPU replacement. No structural parallelism to exploit in the composition itself. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Smoothers/DILUGaussSeidel/DILUGaussSeidelSmoother.C` |
| `DILUPreconditioner` | cuSPARSE | Asymmetric ILU(0). Forward sweep walks faces through losortPtr (neighbour-sorted order) and the backward sweep walks raw face order; both carry the wA dependency. preconditionT swaps which sweep uses losort. Maps onto two cusparseSpSV analyses (L and U) per matrix. | Sequential triangular substitution in both directions, plus the losort indirection which destroys coalescing (gather through losortPtr then through uPtr/lPtr - a double indirection per face). calcReciprocalD is sequential and runs per solve in the constructor. preconditionT means BiCG needs a second pair of analyses. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Preconditioners/DILUPreconditioner/DILUPreconditioner.C` |
| `DILUSmoother` | cuSPARSE | Asymmetric counterpart of DICSmoother: residual (A-tier), DILU forward/backward substitution through losort (sequential), psi += rA (A-tier). Same level-scheduling treatment as DILUPreconditioner. | Sequential substitution plus the losort double indirection which wrecks coalescing. Sequential calcReciprocalD in the constructor. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Smoothers/DILU/DILUSmoother.C` |
| `FDICPreconditioner` | cuSPARSE | Same DIC factorisation and the same two sequential sweeps, but with rDuUpper_/rDlUpper_ pre-multiplied so the inner loop is one multiply instead of two. The pre-scaling loops themselves are pure elementwise (A-tier); the substitution is unchanged and still loop-carried. | Identical sequential forward/backward dependency to DIC. Extra 2*nFaces of device memory for the pre-scaled arrays - on a 16 GB card that matters at large mesh sizes. Constructor (including the sequential calcReciprocalD) reruns per solve. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Preconditioners/FDICPreconditioner/FDICPreconditioner.C` |
| `FDICSmoother` | cuSPARSE | Identical to DICSmoother with rDuUpper_/rDlUpper_ pre-scaled, saving one multiply per face in each substitution sweep. Extra device memory for the pre-scaled arrays with no GPU-side benefit, since the kernel is memory-bound not ALU-bound. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Smoothers/FDIC/FDICSmoother.C` |
| `GAMGPreconditioner` | AMGX | Derives from GAMGSolver, so it inherits the entire hierarchy-rebuild-per-construction problem, and then runs nVcycles V-cycles per preconditioning call - i.e. per Krylov iteration. The between-cycle work (Amul then r - AwA) is A-tier, everything expensive is the V-cycle. | Everything listed under GAMGSolver, amplified: the coarsest-level IStringStream dictionary parse now happens nVcycles times per Krylov iteration. Use AMGX's AMG-preconditioned Krylov instead of porting this. | XL | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Preconditioners/GAMGPreconditioner/GAMGPreconditioner.C` |
| `GAMGSolver` | AMGX | The V-cycle itself is all GPU-shaped work - restrict (segmented scatter-add), prolong (gather), Amul per level, scale (two reductions), interpolate. Replace wholesale with AMGX aggregation AMG rather than porting. | agglomerateMatrix() is called from the constructor for every level on every solve - it allocates a new lduMatrix, new PtrList<lduInterfaceField>, new FieldField bou/int coeff arrays per level, per solve. solveCoarsestLevel() calls PCGsolverDict()/PBiCGStabSolverDict() which construct a dictionary by PARSING THE STRING "solver PCG; preconditioner DIC;" through an IStringStream on every V-cycle - a tokeniser run in the innermost time-loop path. initVcycle() calls lduMatrix::smoother::New per level per solve. Under processorAgglomerate the constructor round-trips GAMGInterfaces through OStringStream/IStringStream. | XL | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Solvers/GAMG/GAMGSolver.C` |
| `GaussSeidelSmoother` | AMGX | The cell loop is inherently serial: for each cell it reads bPrime[celli], accumulates -upper*psi[uPtr[face]] over its owner faces, divides by diag, writes psi[celli], then IMMEDIATELY scatters bPrime[uPtr[face]] -= lower*psii into cells it has not yet visited. Cell i+1 therefore consumes what cell i just wrote. There is no way to parallelise this while preserving the iterate sequence. The GPU answer is AMGX's multicolour Gauss-Seidel or a polynomial/Chebyshev smoother. | Loop-carried dependency through bPrime, which is a true serial chain of length nCells. Multicolouring restores parallelism but CHANGES the numerics (same fixed point, different iterates), so residual histories will not match the CPU run - budget for revalidation, not bit-comparison. Also mutates interfaceBouCoeffs_ in place via const_cast negate()/negate() around the sweep, which is not thread-safe and must be replaced by a sign flag on device. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Smoothers/GaussSeidel/GaussSeidelSmoother.C` |
| `LLTMatrix<Type>` | cuSOLVER | Cholesky A = L L' on a symmetric positive-definite SquareMatrix plus forward/back substitution. Sizes far too small to amortise a GPU library call; do it in one CUDA block with the data already resident, or accept a single tiny D2H at the coarsest level only. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LUscalarMatrix/LUscalarMatrix.C` |

### D-hard  <sub>(6)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `SVD` | cuSOLVER | Householder bidiagonalisation followed by QR iteration - a data-dependent convergence loop with heavy branching, deflation tests and a nZeros_ counter driven by a singular-value threshold. Faithfully porting this iteration is a research exercise; the practical route is cuSOLVER gesvd, or for the small least-squares matrices it actually serves, replacing SVDinv with a batched QR or normal-equations solve. | Iterative QR sweeps with convergence tests on singular values (branch-divergent, unpredictable trip count), Householder reflector chains that are sequential in the column index, and deflation logic. Matrices are tiny, so cuSOLVER gesvd is pure overhead and batched gesvdjBatched only covers small fixed sizes. | XL | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/scalarMatrices/SVD/SVD.C` |
| `algebraicPairGAMGAgglomeration` | none | The weight computation is A-tier (faceWeights = mag(upper) or mag(upper+lower)/2, normalised by a max-reduce - one elementwise kernel plus one CUB reduce), but it then feeds the sequential pairGAMGAgglomeration matching, which dominates and is serial. | Inherits the serial greedy matching. Worse than faceAreaPair for GPU purposes because the weights depend on the MATRIX coefficients, so if the matrix changes and cacheAgglomeration is off the whole serial coarsening re-runs per solve - a full host round-trip inside the time loop. Keep cacheAgglomeration on, or prefer faceAreaPair. | XL | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Solvers/GAMG/GAMGAgglomerations/algebraicPairGAMGAgglomeration/algebraicPairGAMGAgglomeration.C` |
| `faceAreaPairGAMGAgglomeration` | none | Weights are mag(Sf)/max(mag(Sf)) - purely geometric, so for a static mesh they never change and the coarsening is computed exactly once and cached in the mesh registry. The pairing algorithm itself is the same serial greedy matching. Being the DEFAULT agglomerator, this is the one that actually matters. | Serial greedy matching from pairGAMGAgglomeration. Mitigated entirely on a static mesh: run at setup on the host, upload the restriction addressing, never touch it again - which satisfies the zero-round-trip bar. Only becomes a live blocker for moving/topo-changing meshes. Note it lives in libfiniteVolume, not src/OpenFOAM, because it needs fvMesh face areas. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/finiteVolume/fvMatrices/solvers/GAMGSymSolver/GAMGAgglomerations/faceAreaPairGAMGAgglomeration/faceAreaPairGAMGAgglomeration.C` |
| `nonBlockingGaussSeidelSmoother` | none | Its only reason to exist is overlapping the MPI halo exchange with interior smoothing - it computes blockStart_ = min over all coupled patches' faceCells, smooths cells below that index while sends are in flight, then waits and smooths the rest. On a single RTX 5070 Ti with no MPI there is nothing to overlap, so the entire mechanism is dead weight on top of an already-serial Gauss-Seidel. | MPI-dependent by construction (initInterfaceMatrixUpdate then waitRequests around the blockStart_ split). REQUIRES the mesh to have been renumbered so all coupled cells sort last - a global constraint on cell ordering that fights GPU-friendly renumbering (multicolour, level-set, space-filling curve). Plus the serial GS chain. Do not port; select GaussSeidel instead. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Smoothers/nonBlockingGaussSeidel/nonBlockingGaussSeidelSmoother.C` |
| `pairGAMGAgglomeration` | none | Sequential greedy graph matching. The cell loop reads and writes coarseCellMap in visit order and increments a running nCoarseCells counter - cell i's cluster assignment depends on every cell before it having already claimed its partner (the test is coarseCellMap[upperAddr[facei]] < 0 && coarseCellMap[lowerAddr[facei]] < 0). A parallel maximal matching (Luby, handshaking) produces a DIFFERENT and generally worse coarsening, so this is a research port, not a translation. | Loop-carried coarseCellMap plus a running counter. Alternating sweep direction per level via forward_ flag, then a full renumbering pass if !forward_. combineLevels/compactLevels reshuffle the level arrays. Practically: leave it on the host, run it once at setup (it is cached), and upload only restrictAddressing/faceRestrictAddressing. | XL | `[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/matrices/LduMatrix/Solvers/GAMG/GAMGAgglomerations/pairGAMGAgglomeration/pairGAMGAgglomeration.C` |
| `pairPatchAgglomeration` | none | Greedy pair agglomeration on a surface patch, driven by an EdgeMap<scalar> of shared-edge-length weights (confirmed forAllConstIter over EdgeMap<scalar> facePairWeight_) with a featureAngle constraint. Same sequential greedy-matching character as the volume agglomerators, on top of a hash map keyed by edge. | EdgeMap hash lookups per face pair, sequential greedy matching with a running cluster counter, featureAngle branching. ## Momentum transport / turbulence

### A-trivial  <sub>(62)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |

| `DeardorffDiffStress` | AMGX | Transports the full six-component SGS stress R with a Daly-Harlow tensor diffusivity (I*nu + Cs*(k/epsilon)*R), so it needs the same anisotropic laplacian assembly as LRR/SSG. k = 0.5*tr(R) and epsilon = Ce*k^1.5/delta are elementwise. | Six-component sparse solve with a tensor diffusion coefficient -- needs a device fvm::laplacian for symmTensor coefficients including the non-orthogonal correction. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/LES/DeardorffDiffStress/DeardorffDiffStress.H` |
| `Giesekus` | AMGX | Reuses the whole Maxwell correct() machinery and only overrides sigmaSource() to add -(alphaG/nuM)*(sigma & sigma) alongside the fvm::Sp relaxation. The extra term is an elementwise symmTensor inner product; the sparse symmTensor solves still dominate. | Same as Maxwell: the per-mode rLambda IOobject construction inside the time loop, and nModes sparse solves per step. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/laminar/Giesekus/Giesekus.H` |
| `LRR` | AMGX | One epsilon solve plus a six-component R solve. The Daly-Harlow diffusivity DREff = Cs*(k/eps)*R + nu*I makes the diffusion coefficient a full symmTensor, so the laplacian becomes an anisotropic face assembly (Sf & interpolate(DREff) & Sf / magSf^2 plus a non-orthogonal correction) -- heavier per face but still a static owner/neighbour stencil. Wall reflection uses wallDist and the patch normal, elementwise. | Sparse solves dominate. The anisotropic laplacian needs a device implementation of fvm::laplacian with a tensor coefficient including the explicit non-orthogonal correction. correctWallShearStress does host isA<wallFvPatch> RTTI -- pre-classify at setup. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/RAS/LRR/LRR.H` |
| `LaheyKEpsilon` | AMGX | Two sparse solves plus bubble-induced production terms and the Sato-like nutAlphaEff, all elementwise on the phase fields. The complication is not arithmetic but plumbing: it reaches the dispersed phase through refCast<const phaseModel>(this->properties()) and registry lookups on the phaseSystem every step. | Per-step host refCast/registry traversal to reach the other phase's alpha, d and U (O(nPhases), not per-cell, but it is host orchestration inside the loop). Only registered in the multiphaseEuler module, so the whole Euler-Euler solver has to be device-resident first. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/phaseCompressible/RAS/LaheyKEpsilon/LaheyKEpsilon.H` |

The very long list of transition functions (fSS shear-sheltering, fW, fTaul, Rbp/Rnat bypass and natural production, the small/large-scale energy split) is one enormous but entirely elementwise exp/pow/min/max expression tree over cell fields. | Three sparse solves per step. | `lambdaThixotropic` | AMGX | correct() assembles and solves one scalar transport matrix for lambda_ (ddt + div == a*(1-lambda)^b - fvm::Sp(c*strainRate^d + residualAlpha term)), then clamps with lambda_.maxMin(0,1) and evaluates nu = nuInf/(1-K*lambda)^2 elementwise. The solve dominates; everything else is elementwise pow/max. | One sparse solve per step. Nothing else -- no indirection, no host containers. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/laminar/lambdaThixotropic/lambdaThixotropic.H` |
| `mixtureKEpsilon` | AMGX | Two sparse solves on the mixture fields (epsEqn at line 663, kmEqn at line 686), with rhom, Ur and the response coefficient Ct built elementwise, then k and epsilon of each phase reconstructed. Grep shows seven separate refCast<const phaseModel>(this->properties()) sites plus a U.db().lookupObject call. | The heaviest host plumbing in the subsystem: repeated refCast and objectRegistry lookups per step to assemble the mixture from both phases, plus it requires the special epsilonmWallFunction. multiphaseEuler-module-only. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/phaseCompressible/RAS/mixtureKEpsilon/mixtureKEpsilon.H` |
| `qZeta` | AMGX | Two sparse solves for q_ and zeta_ (with k = q^2, epsilon = 2*q*zeta). boundZeta() is an elementwise max against a floor. The low-Re damping fMu/f2 are exps, and the E term uses fvc::magSqrGradGrad -- a chained double gradient, i.e. two Gauss stencils. | Two sparse solves per step. magSqrGradGrad needs a 9-component tensor intermediate and a second gradient pass. Incompressible library only. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/incompressible/RAS/qZeta/qZeta.H` |
| `realizableKE` | AMGX | Two sparse solves plus a variable Cmu built from strain-rate invariants: As = sqrt(6)*cos(phi) where phi = (1/3)*acos(min(max(sqrt(6)*W,-1),1)) from the third invariant of S. That is trigonometry on cell fields -- transcendental-heavy but perfectly elementwise and branch-free thanks to the min/max clamping. | Two sparse solves per step. The acos/cos chain is elementwise; no host dependency. | M | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/RAS/realizableKE/realizableKE.H` |
| `v2f` | AMGX | Grepped the source: exactly four solve() calls per step -- epsEqn, kEqn, fEqn (the elliptic relaxation, -fvm::laplacian(f) - fvm::Sp(1/L^2, f)) and v2Eqn. No forAll loops at all in v2f.C; everything between the solves is elementwise (Ts, L, Ceps1 = 1.4*(1+0.05*sqrt(k/v2)), nut = min(CmuKEps*k^2/eps, Cmu*v2*Ts)). | Four sparse solves per step. The f equation is a pure Helmholtz/elliptic system -- stiffer than the convection-dominated ones, so it wants a real multigrid (AMGX) rather than a simple Krylov+ILU. Needs the v2WallFunction/fWallFunction pair, which reach the model through refCast<v2fBase>. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/RAS/v2f/v2f.H` |

### D-hard  <sub>(4)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `MaxwellStefan` | cuSOLVER | I read transformDiffusionCoefficientFields(): it loops over EVERY cell and patch face, and for each one gathers Y and the binary coefficients DD from PtrList<PtrList<volScalarField>> indirection, builds the (N-1)x(N-1) A and B matrices with index-skipping counters (is/js skip the default specie), then calls A.decompose(); A.inv(invA); multiply(D, invA, B). That is a dense LU factorisation and explicit inverse per cell, performed on shared mutable scratch scalarSquareMatrix members. | Per-cell dense LU + explicit inverse using host-heap mutable static scratch matrices shared across the loop -- inherently serial as written. A device version needs batched dense LU (cuSOLVER getrfBatched/getriBatched, or a register-blocked kernel for small N) and a full rewrite of the PtrList<PtrList<volScalarField>> layout into a flat [nCells x N x N] device array. The is/js index-skipping and the runtime-variable N add branchiness. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/ThermophysicalTransportModels/fluid/laminar/MaxwellStefan/MaxwellStefan.H` |
| `MaxwellStefanFourier` | cuSOLVER | Inherits the whole per-cell dense Maxwell-Stefan matrix inversion and adds Fourier conduction plus the implicit energy correction. The Fourier half is trivially A-tier; the model's tier is set entirely by the inherited per-cell LU/inverse. | Everything listed for MaxwellStefan: per-cell batched dense LU, mutable static scratch matrices, PtrList<PtrList<volScalarField>> layout. Multicomponent libraries only. | L | `[Foundation-12] src/ThermophysicalTransportModels/fluid/laminar/MaxwellStefanFourier/MaxwellStefanFourier.H` |
| `WallLocationYPlus` | none | Not a computational kernel but an FvFaceCellWave transport type: a small struct with update() methods that decide whether an incoming wall location supersedes the current one, carrying origin point and yStar, and cutting the wave off past yPlusCutOff. Its whole reason to exist is the sequential, order-dependent front propagation it rides on. | It is the payload of an inherently sequential wave algorithm -- the update() semantics are order-dependent (later arrivals overwrite conditionally), which is precisely what a parallel front cannot reproduce without a different formulation. Only reachable through vanDriestDelta. | XL | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/LES/LESdeltas/vanDriestDelta/WallLocationYPlus.H` |
| `vanDriestDelta` | none | Unlike every other delta, correct() runs EVERY time step by default (calcInterval_ defaults to 1) and calls fvPatchDistWave::calculateAndCorrect<WallLocationYPlus>. That is an iterative face-cell front propagation over the unstructured mesh carrying a WallLocationYPlus payload, with a data-dependent number of sweeps and, in parallel, MPI halo exchange per sweep. It also constructs fresh yStar and y volScalarFields with IOobjects each call. | In-loop wave propagation with dynamic sweep counts and irregular front growth -- exactly the pattern that does not map to a GPU. Plus per-call IOobject-registered field construction and, in parallel, Pstream exchange per sweep. Either keep the whole delta on the host (a full field round-trip every step, violating the bar) or replace it with a GPU-friendly iterative Jacobi/Eikonal wall-distance sweep. Simplest mitigation: raise calcInterval or pick a different delta. | XL | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/LES/LESdeltas/vanDriestDelta/vanDriestDelta.H` |

### E-cpu-only  <sub>(23)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `LESModel` | none | Same scaffolding as RASModel plus ownership of the runtime-selected LESdelta. Critically, LESModel::correct() calls delta_().correct() EVERY time step -- so whichever delta is selected runs in the time loop, and its tier propagates here. | delta_().correct() in the time loop: harmless for cubeRootVol/maxDeltaxyz/IDDES (they no-op on a static mesh) but a hard host round-trip for vanDriestDelta, which reruns fvPatchDistWave every step by default (calcInterval 1). | S | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/LES/LESModel/LESModel.H` |
| `LESThermophysicalTransportModel` | none | Reads the 'LES' sub-dictionary and provides the LES selection table. Interface and registration only. | `LESdelta` | none | Abstract base holding the delta_ volScalarField and the dictionary runtime-selection table. It owns no arithmetic; correct() is pure virtual. The virtual correct() called every step from LESModel::correct() is the dispatch point that must be resolved to a concrete device path at setup. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/LES/LESdeltas/LESdelta/LESdelta.H` |
| `LESfilter` | none | Abstract base with the dictionary runtime-selection table and four pure-virtual operator() overloads (scalar, vector, symmTensor, tensor). | `PhaseThermophysicalTransportModel` | none | The per-phase counterpart of ThermophysicalTransportModel, instantiated for (phaseCompressibleMomentumTransportModel, fluidThermo) and (..., fluidMulticomponentThermo). Template and registration scaffolding only. | None in the time loop, but it commits you to the phaseSystem, whose per-step host lookups are the real multiphase obstacle. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/ThermophysicalTransportModels/fluid/PhaseThermophysicalTransportModel/PhaseThermophysicalTransportModel.H` |
| `RASModel` | none | Selection/bounding scaffolding: reads turbulence_/printCoeffs_ switches and kMin_/epsilonMin_/omegaMin_ at construction, optionally constructs a generalisedNewtonianViscosityModel, and overrides nu() to return it. The runtime-selection table and the optional viscosityModel construction are setup. nu() returning a fresh tmp<volScalarField> per call is an allocation pattern to eliminate, not a correctness blocker. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/RAS/RASModel/RASModel.H` |
| `RASThermophysicalTransportModel` | none | Reads the 'RAS' sub-dictionary and provides the RAS selection table for thermophysical transport. Interface and registration only. | Dictionary parsing at construction only. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/ThermophysicalTransportModels/fluid/turbulence/RAS/RASThermophysicalTransportModel/RASThermophysicalTransportModel.H` |
| `ThermophysicalTransportModel` | none | Templated on (momentum transport model, thermo package); carries the laminar/RAS/LES runtime-selection tables. Instantiated for (compressibleMomentumTransportModel, fluidThermo) and (..., fluidMulticomponentThermo). Pure template and registration scaffolding. | None in the time loop. The thermo-package template argument determines which models are even available -- a compile-time decision the port must mirror. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/ThermophysicalTransportModels/fluid/ThermophysicalTransportModel/ThermophysicalTransportModel.H` |
| `compressibleMomentumTransportModel` | none | Same instantiation vehicle with alpha=geometricOneField, rho=volScalarField. Adds no arithmetic of its own -- it only fixes which fields the derived models multiply through, and owns the library registration for libcompressibleMomentumTransportModels. | None in the time loop; template/registration scaffolding only. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/compressible/compressibleMomentumTransportModel.H` |
| `externalCoupledTemperature` | none | Grepped the source: OFstream/IFstream plain-text exchange in $FOAM_CASE/<commsDir>, Pstream::gatherList of magSf, T, qDot and htc onto the master, a lock file, and a wait loop bounded by waitInterval/timeOut before reading refValue/refGrad/valueFraction back. The BC exists specifically to block on an external process. | Disk I/O, a lock-file handshake with an external application, an MPI gather to the master, and a blocking wait -- an unavoidable host round-trip every calcFrequency steps. This is the single hardest violation of the zero-round-trip bar in the subsystem and cannot be ported; it can only be scheduled (download the patch fields, exchange, upload). | S | `[Foundation-12] src/ThermophysicalTransportModels/fluid/derivedFvPatchFields/externalCoupledTemperatureMixed/externalCoupledTemperatureMixedFvPatchScalarField.H` |
| `fluidThermophysicalTransportModel` | none | Abstract base providing the common j()/divj() specie-flux interface and alphaEff; the default j(Yi) = -rho*DEff(Yi)*snGrad(Yi) is a face stencil but the class is otherwise an interface. | `incompressibleMomentumTransportModel` | none | Typedef-level base: instantiates the template chain with alpha=geometricOneField, rho=geometricOneField. geometricOneField is a compile-time no-op that folds out of every expression, so on the device this flavour is the cheapest -- but the class itself contains no kernels, only the template plumbing and the library-registration macros. | None in the time loop. It is a compile-time instantiation vehicle; the GPU port replaces it with a template/tag-dispatch that folds alpha and rho to 1. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/incompressible/incompressibleMomentumTransportModel.H` |
| `laminarModel` | none | Reads the 'laminar' sub-dictionary and <type>Coeffs at construction, provides zero-valued k()/epsilon()/omega()/nut() tmp fields, and forwards correct() to the base. laminarModel::correct() is a single virtual call with no arithmetic. | Dictionary reads happen in the constructor and in read(); nothing is parsed inside the time loop. | `laminarThermophysicalTransportModel` | none | Reads the 'laminar' sub-dictionary of constant/thermophysicalTransport with a plain lookup("model") (no backwards-compatible alias here) and provides the selection table. No arithmetic. | Dictionary parsing at construction only. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/ThermophysicalTransportModels/fluid/laminar/laminarThermophysicalTransportModel/laminarThermophysicalTransportModel.H` |
| `momentumTransportModel` | none | Pure abstract interface deriving from IOdictionary; readModelDict() parses constant/momentumTransport (with the legacy turbulenceProperties fallback and <phaseName> group suffix handling) and New() drives the runtime-selection HashTable. Everything else is object-registry plumbing. | IOdictionary parsing, runtime-selection table, objectRegistry lookups -- all construction-time only, so it does not violate the zero-round-trip bar. divDevTauCorr allocates and registers a named surfaceVectorField 'devTauCorrFlux' on every momentum assembly; that registration must be replaced by a pre-allocated device buffer. | S | `[Foundation-12] src/MomentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModels/momentumTransportModel.H` |
| `nutWallFunctionFvPatchScalarField` | none | Base class only -- no makePatchTypeField, so it cannot be a patch type. No arithmetic of substance here. | None in the time loop, but this flavour drags in the whole phaseSystem (per-phase PtrList/HashTable lookups, refCast<phaseModel> of the viscosity object) which the derived multiphase models exercise every step. | `phaseIncompressibleMomentumTransportModel` | none | Instantiation vehicle with alpha=volScalarField, rho=geometricOneField. | `phaseSolidThermophysicalTransportModel` | none | Per-phase counterpart for solid phases in multiphaseEuler, with the same isotropic/anisotropic selection. | `smoothDelta` | none | setChangedFaces() builds DynamicList<labelPair> and DynamicList<deltaData> by append() while scanning every internal face and every coupled-patch face, then FvFaceCellWave propagates the ratio constraint with mesh.globalData().nTotalCells()+1 max iterations. ## Thermophysical and chemistry

### A-trivial  <sub>(156)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `APIdiffCoefFunc` | none | Closed-form Function2 of (p,T): one pow(1.8*T, 1.75), one sqrt and a divide on constant molecular weights and volumes. | Virtual Function2::value() when reached through the generic path. | S | `src/thermophysicalModels/specie/thermophysicalFunctions/APIdiffCoef` |
| `AndradeTransport` | none | log(mu) = c0 + c1*T + c2*T^2 + c3/(c4 + T) then exp; closed form, five coefficients. | — | S | `src/thermophysicalModels/specie/transport/Andrade` |

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |

| `hydrostaticInitialisation` | AMGX | hydrostaticInitialisation.C:82-106 runs `for (i=0; i<nCorr; i++)` solving fvm::laplacian(rhof, ph_rgh) == fvc::div(phig) — a Poisson solve repeated nHydrostaticCorrectors times, then updates p and rho. | Runs once before the time loop and never on restart, so the host round-trip here costs nothing against the zero-round-trip bar; Info<< printing per corrector; needs the same AMG setup as the pressure equation. | M | `src/thermophysicalModels/basic/fluidThermo/hydrostaticInitialisation.C` |

| `DAC` | custom-kernels | Uses the DRGEP rAB matrix seeded from a user search-initiating set (fuel, HO2, CO) and propagates interaction coefficients through the species graph per cell. | Same per-cell graph traversal, dynamic containers and search-set lookups as DRGEP; mutates the active-species count used by the ODE solver. | XL | `src/thermophysicalModels/chemistryModel/chemistryModel/reduction/DAC` |
| `DRG` | custom-kernels | Builds the directed relation graph rAB per cell from the current reaction rates and then does a graph reachability search from the search-initiating set, marking species active. | Per-cell graph construction and breadth-first traversal with a work queue; the active set changes the ODE system size per cell so downstream batching becomes ragged; runs inside the cell loop, i.e. inside the time loop. | XL | `src/thermophysicalModels/chemistryModel/chemistryModel/reduction/DRG` |
| `DRGEP` | custom-kernels | DRGEP.C:150-268 builds DynamicList<scalar> wA and DynamicList<label> wAID per reaction with append() inside `while(!usedIndex.empty())` loops; lines 338-500 maintain DynamicList queues QStart/alphaQ, run `while (!Q.empty())` propagation, and at line 470 constructs a SortableListDRGEP and sorts it to pick the NGroupBased lowest R values — all per cell, per time step. | DynamicList growth (heap allocation) per cell; sorting per cell; queue-based graph propagation with data-dependent trip counts; nested while loops over a mutable usedIndex set. Nothing here maps to a device kernel without a complete algorithmic redesign. | XL | `src/thermophysicalModels/chemistryModel/chemistryModel/reduction/DRGEP` |
| `EFA` | custom-kernels | Element Flux Analysis computes element fluxes between species pairs for every reaction and then ranks them using the SortableListEFA helper to decide which species survive. | Per-cell sorting (SortableListEFA) and pairwise flux accumulation over a ragged reaction/species structure; dynamic containers; runs inside the time loop. | XL | `src/thermophysicalModels/chemistryModel/chemistryModel/reduction/EFA` |

| `ISAT` | none | ISAT.C:310-600 walks a heap-allocated binary tree per cell, computes LUA.inv(A) at line 392, and when the tree is full copies every leaf into a `DynamicList<chemPointISAT*> tempList` with `new chemPointISAT(*iter())` at lines 539-552 before rebalancing. chemPointISAT.C:258 runs an SVD per added point and lines 549-613 grow the ellipsoid of accuracy with DynamicList<label> dimToAdd plus matrix row/column insertion. | Pointer-chasing binary tree with new/delete per cell inside the time loop; SVD and matrix inversion per added point; DynamicList growth; the tree is inherently sequential (later cells see a tree modified by earlier cells), which destroys the parallelism the GPU needs. Recommend disabling tabulation entirely on a GPU-native path. | XL | `src/thermophysicalModels/chemistryModel/chemistryModel/tabulation/ISAT` |

| `PFA` | custom-kernels | Computes first- and second-generation production/consumption flux ratios r1/r2 per species pair, i.e. a two-hop graph product, then thresholds them per cell. | Two-hop graph traversal with dynamic lists per cell; data-dependent active-species count feeding the ODE system. | XL | `src/thermophysicalModels/chemistryModel/chemistryModel/reduction/PFA` |
| `Reaction` | custom-kernels | Reaction.C:290-545 computes Cf/Cr as products of pow(max(c[si],0), exponent) over the ragged lhs/rhs specieCoeffs lists, then omega = kf*Cf - kr*Cr and scatters into dNdtByV and the Jacobian ddNdtByVdcTp. Reaction.H declares kf, kr, dkfdT, dkrdT, hasDkdc, dkfdc, dkrdc as pure virtual, so every rate evaluation is an indirect call. | PtrList of polymorphic Reaction objects with host vtables; ragged per-reaction species lists (variable-length inner loops => warp divergence); pow() with run-time exponents; scatter-add into dNdtByV means atomics if reactions are parallelised. | `Rosenbrock12` | cuBLAS | Two stages, each requiring a solve of (I/(gamma*dx) - J)k_i = rhs against the analytic dense Jacobian; one LUDecompose per step plus two back-substitutions. | Dense (nSpecie+2)^2 factorisation per cell per step; mutable a_/dfdy_/pivotIndices_ members shared across cells; Jacobian storage for all cells exceeds 16 GB for realistic mechanisms so cells must be batched; divergent adaptive stepping. | L | `src/ODE/ODESolvers/Rosenbrock12` |
| `Rosenbrock23` | cuBLAS | Three Rosenbrock stages sharing one LU factorisation of (I/(gamma*dx) - J) per step, with an embedded 2nd-order solution for error control. | Same dense batched LU, shared mutable matrix workspace, per-cell Jacobian memory pressure and divergent stepping as Rosenbrock12. | L | `src/ODE/ODESolvers/Rosenbrock23` |
| `Rosenbrock34` | cuBLAS | Four Rosenbrock stages against the analytic Jacobian, one factorisation and four back-substitutions per step. | Dense batched LU per cell per step; shared mutable a_/dfdy_ members; Jacobian memory forces cell tiling; divergent adaptive stepping. | L | `src/ODE/ODESolvers/Rosenbrock34` |
| `SIBS` | cuBLAS | Semi-implicit Bader-Deuflhard midpoint with Bulirsch-Stoer extrapolation: a variable-length sequence of sub-steps, each needing a linear solve, followed by polynomial extrapolation over a tableau whose depth is chosen at run time from the observed error. | Number of sub-steps and extrapolation depth are data-dependent per cell (extreme warp divergence); repeated dense LU per cell; extrapolation tableau is a resizable per-cell 2-D workspace held as mutable members; step and order control are inherently serial. | XL | `src/ODE/ODESolvers/SIBS` |

## Multiphase, two-phase, lagrangian, waves

### A-trivial  <sub>(118)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |

| `waveModels::solitary` | custom-kernels | eta = a*sech^2(...) and a velocity polynomial in Z and Pi with a tanh factor - all elementwise over the coordinate fields. | — | S | `src/waves/waveModels/solitary/solitary.H` |

### B-sparse-solve  <sub>(1)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PackingModels::Implicit` | AMGX | Assembles and SOLVES a real fvScalarMatrix on the Eulerian mesh - fvm::d2dt2(alpha) - fvm::laplacian(tauPrimeByRhoAf, alpha) (+ fvm::div when phiGByA is present) - then alphaEqn.solve() and maps alphaEqn.flux() back onto the parcels. | The solve itself is the classic AMGX target, but it is wrapped in five correctBoundaryConditions() calls, a parcel->mesh scatter to build alpha_p and a mesh->parcel gather of the correction flux, plus fvc::reconstruct of phiCorrect_. The linear system is device-friendly; the Lagrangian mapping around it is not. | L | `src/lagrangian/parcel/submodels/MPPIC/PackingModels/Implicit/Implicit.H` |

### C-fft  <sub>(7)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `OUprocess` | cuFFT | newField() is OUfield_[i] = (1-alpha*dt)*OUfield_[i] + scale*sigma*W(dt) inside a band-limit test kLower<\|k\|<kUpper - a masked elementwise AR(1) update over the wavenumber field. | Six stdNormal_.sample() draws per active mode from a host RNG in a serial forAll; replace with cuRAND (curand_normal2) per thread. State OUfield_ must persist on device between steps. | M | `src/randomProcesses/processes/OUprocess/OUprocess.H` |
| `fft` | cuFFT | A hand-rolled Numerical Recipes multi-dimensional Danielson-Lanczos butterfly over a raw scalar* aliased from complexField, with bit-reversal SWAPs and nested strided loops. Functionally a 1-3D power-of-2 C2C transform. | Delete and replace with cuFFT rather than port: the code reinterpret_casts complexField to scalar*, uses 1-based Fortran indexing, and asserts every dimension is a power of two. Note cuFFT's normalisation differs - this one applies 1/sqrt(N) symmetrically in BOTH directions. | M | `src/randomProcesses/fft/fft.H` |
| `fftRenumber` | cuFFT | fftshift: recursive/strided index remap so zero wavenumber sits at the array centre. A pure permutation kernel, or fold into the cuFFT consumer's index arithmetic. | Implemented as a recursive helper over dimensions; on device express it as an index transform rather than a data movement. | S | `src/randomProcesses/fft/fftRenumber.H` |
| `fv::OUForce` | cuFFT | addSup() does ReImSum(fft::reverseTransform((K/(\|K\|+eps)) ^ forceGen_.newField(dt), K.nn())) every time step - a solenoidal projection in wavenumber space followed by a 3-D inverse complex FFT on a power-of-2 box. Exactly cuFFT's C2C 3-D plan plus one elementwise cross-product kernel. | Serial-only by construction (no MPI decomposition of the FFT) and requires an isotropic power-of-2 mesh; the in-house fft must be replaced by cuFFT and the complexVectorField relaid out as interleaved/planar device memory. write() calls writeEk (extra FFTs) at write times only. | M | `src/randomProcesses/OUForce/OUForce.H` |
| `kShellIntegration` | Thrust/CUB | kShellMean loops all N modes and, for each, scans NoSubintervals shells testing \|k\| against a delta_k window - an O(N*nShells) weighted histogram, then scales by 4*pi*k^2 and box factors. | The inner shell scan should become a direct bin index plus atomicAdd (or a CUB segmented reduce); as written it is a nested serial loop. Runs at write time only, so it is not on the zero-round-trip critical path. | S | `src/randomProcesses/fft/kShellIntegration.H` |
| `turbGen` | cuFFT | Draws random unit vectors and phases per mode, projects solenoidal via s = K^s normalised, scales by Ek(\|K\|), then one inverse 3-D FFT and ReImSum. Classic synthetic-turbulence field synthesis. | rndGen_.sample01<vector>() and scalar01() in a serial forAll (replace with cuRAND); in-house fft (replace with cuFFT). Used by boxTurb initialisation, so it runs once before the time loop - low priority for a GPU-native path. | S | `src/randomProcesses/turbulence/turbGen.H` |
| `writeEk` | cuFFT | Forward FFT of the three velocity components followed by kShellIntegration, then writes a graph. Spectral post-processing. | Write-time only, and it ends in file output - a D2H of the 1-D spectrum here is legitimate. Uses the in-house fft. | S | `src/randomProcesses/fft/writeEk.H` |

| `SinglePhaseMixture` | custom-kernels | Single-phase multi-component parcel composition (e.g. a multi-component droplet); a per-parcel Y array with per-component property evaluation. | Inherits CompositionModel's name-keyed mapping and per-component virtual property calls; per-parcel variable-length Y. | L | `src/lagrangian/parcel/submodels/Reacting/CompositionModel/SinglePhaseMixture/SinglePhaseMixture.H` |
| `SprayCloud` | custom-kernels | Adds atomisation and secondary breakup, which SPAWN child parcels mid-step - i.e. the cloud population mutates during the move loop. | Dynamic parcel creation during iteration (list insertion while traversing) is the hardest possible pattern for a fixed device buffer; needs a compact-and-append scheme with a device free list. Inherits all ReactingCloud blockers. | XL | `src/lagrangian/parcel/clouds/Templates/SprayCloud/SprayCloud.H` |
| `SprayParcel` | custom-kernels | Adds atomisation/breakup state (d0, liquidCore, KHindex, y, yDot, tc, ms, injector) and integrates the TAB oscillator ODE per parcel. | The TAB ODE itself is A-tier arithmetic, but breakup spawns child parcels mid-loop and the state vector is large; inherits every ReactingParcel blocker. | XL | `src/lagrangian/parcel/parcels/Templates/SprayParcel/SprayParcel.H` |
| `SuppressionCollision` | custom-kernels | Inter-CLOUD model: looks up another named cloud in the registry, forms its volume fraction field, and toggles canReact on parcels of this cloud where the suppressing cloud is dense. | Cross-cloud registry lookup by name each step plus a per-parcel boolean state flip that then changes the reaction branch - divergence and cross-container dependency. | L | `src/lagrangian/parcel/submodels/ReactingMultiphase/StochasticCollision/SuppressionCollision/SuppressionCollision.H` |
| `ThermoCloud` | custom-kernels | Adds per-parcel heat transfer and radiation coupling plus the Eulerian Sh source; the energy balance itself is a handful of flops solved with the integrationScheme. | Requires the carrier thermo (kappa, cp, T interpolation) per parcel, plus radiation field lookups; all reached through the same host virtual per-parcel dispatch. | XL | `src/lagrangian/parcel/clouds/Templates/ThermoCloud/ThermoCloud.H` |
| `ThermoParcel` | custom-kernels | m*cp*dT/dt = htc*A*(Tc-T) + radiation, integrated with the selected scheme - about twenty flops per parcel per sub-step. | Needs Tc, kappac, cpc interpolated at the parcel position (virtual interpolation) and the radiation G field; sits inside the MomentumParcel move loop. | L | `src/lagrangian/parcel/parcels/Templates/ThermoParcel/ThermoParcel.H` |
| `TrajectoryCollision` | custom-kernels | Nordin's extension: detects collisions from the actual relative trajectories (closest approach within dt < r1+r2) rather than same-cell probability, with cSpace/cTime scaling the detection window. | Same O(n^2) in-cell pairing, parcel deletion on coalescence and per-pair RNG as ORourkeCollision, plus extra geometry per pair. | XL | `src/lagrangian/parcel/submodels/Spray/StochasticCollision/TrajectoryCollision/TrajectoryCollision.H` |
| `cloud / Cloud<ParticleType>` | custom-kernels | cloud is an objectRegistry base; Cloud<T> adds IDLList storage, the move() loop, PstreamBuffers-based transfer of particles across processor patches, and topoChange/mapMesh remapping. CloudIO.C does Pstream::listCombineGather/Scatter of particle counts. | Linked-list storage, per-particle new/delete, and an MPI exchange loop that repeats until globally no particle needs transferring. NOTE: Cloud.H/Cloud.C/CloudI.H are ABSENT from this checkout (only cloud.h/cloud.C/CloudIO.C are present) - a case-collision casualty of the NTFS checkout, since OpenFOAM ships both Cloud.H and cloud.H in the same directory. Fix the checkout before attempting anything here. | XL | `src/lagrangian/basic/Cloud/cloud.H` |
| `collidingCloud` | custom-kernels | Concrete instantiation of CollidingCloud; registered in both viscosity and thermo tables. | Identical to CollidingCloud. | S | `src/lagrangian/parcel/clouds/derived/collidingCloud/collidingCloud.H` |
| `correlationFunction / bufferedAccumulator` | custom-kernels | Maintains rolling buffers of a sampled quantity and accumulates C(tau) = <A(t).A(t+tau)> across staggered buffer origins, integrated for Green-Kubo transport coefficients. | Ring-buffer bookkeeping with per-buffer origin offsets and averaging counts - inherently sequential state machine over time, though the inner correlation sums are reducible. Post-processing, not the critical path. | M | `src/lagrangian/molecularDynamics/correlationFunction/correlationFunction.H` |
| `dsmcCloud / DSMCCloud` | custom-kernels | collisions() loops cells, splits each into List<DynamicList<label>> subCells(8), then repeatedly draws candidate pairs with rndGen_.sampleAB and accepts on (sigmaTcR/sigmaTcRMax) > rndGen_.scalar01(), with a no-time-counter candidate budget per cell. Inflow does acceptance-rejection sampling and addNewParcel. | Per-cell DynamicList sub-cell binning rebuilt every step, per-candidate host RNG, particle creation/removal every step, reduce(collisions) each step, and a do{}while acceptance-rejection loop for internal energy. Classic GPU DSMC exists but is a full rewrite. | XL | `src/lagrangian/DSMC/clouds/Templates/DSMCCloud/DSMCCloud.H` |
| `dsmcParcel / DSMCParcel` | custom-kernels | Carries U, Ei and typeId and moves ballistically between stochastic collisions - the free flight itself is x += U*dt, but it goes through particle::track. | Inherits the full barycentric tracking blocker set; parcels are created and destroyed continuously by inflow and boundary escape. | XL | `src/lagrangian/DSMC/parcels/Templates/DSMCParcel/DSMCParcel.H` |
| `functionObjects::particles` | custom-kernels | Constructs a parcel cloud and evolves it each step against a looked-up incompressible U field without affecting the flow - one-way coupled post-processing tracking. | Full Lagrangian tracking cost with all of its blockers; reads rho from constant/phaseProperties and g from constant/g. | XL | `src/lagrangian/functionObjects/particles/particles.H` |
| `fv::clouds` | custom-kernels | correct() evolves every cloud (full Lagrangian tracking) and addSup contributes SU(U), Sh(he), SYi(Yi) and Srho to the Eulerian equations. The addSup half is A-tier matrix arithmetic; correct() is the entire Lagrangian solver. | Reads constant/clouds for the cloud names and g from constant/g; each addSup dispatches on field name. Its GPU tier is dominated by whatever parcelCloudList::evolve does, which is host+MPI today. | XL | `src/lagrangian/parcel/fvModels/clouds/clouds.H` |
| `molecule / moleculeCloud` | custom-kernels | Velocity-Verlet integration with rigid-body rotation (orientation tensor Q, angular momentum), with pair, tether and electrostatic forces evaluated over direct and referred interaction lists. buildCellOccupancy() is called three times per step. | Repeated buildCellOccupancy (List<DynamicList<molecule*>>), PstreamBuffers non-blocking referred-data exchange three times per step, DynamicList<molecule*> molsToDelete accumulation, and forAllIter traversal of IDLLists of referred molecules. GPU MD is a solved problem but in a completely different code structure. | XL | `src/lagrangian/molecularDynamics/molecule/molecule.H` |
| `momentumCloud` | custom-kernels | Concrete typedef instantiating the MomentumCloud template onto momentumParcel; registered as "cloud" in both the viscosity and thermo tables. | Identical to MomentumCloud - this file only pins the template instantiation and RTS registration. | S | `src/lagrangian/parcel/clouds/derived/momentumCloud/momentumCloud.H` |
| `mppicCloud` | custom-kernels | Concrete instantiation of MPPICCloud, registered under the keyword "MPPICCloud" (not the class name) in both tables. | Identical to MPPICCloud. | S | `src/lagrangian/parcel/clouds/derived/mppicCloud/mppicCloud.H` |
| `particle` | custom-kernels | track() is a serial per-particle while(onInternalFace) loop; trackToFace() loops tets with a stuck-detection counter (nTracksBehind_ < maxNTracksBehind_); trackToStationaryTri builds a barycentricTensor, tests four faces and picks the minimum hit fraction. locate() falls back to mesh.cellTree().findInside - an octree search. | Unbounded data-dependent iteration per particle, irregular gather of face/tet connectivity, indexedOctree traversal, and coupled-patch handling that applies a transformer and hands the particle to another processor (MPI). Maximal warp divergence; a device port is a research project. | XL | `src/lagrangian/basic/particle/particle.H` |
| `passiveParticle` | custom-kernels | Adds no state to particle - it is the base barycentric tracking algorithm with nothing else, so it inherits every tracking blocker. | Same as particle. Note passiveParticle.C is not even compiled (not in Make/files); it is header-only. | L | `src/lagrangian/basic/passiveParticle/passiveParticle.H` |
| `passiveParticleCloud` | custom-kernels | Cloud<passiveParticle> - an IDLList of heap-allocated particles with the move loop and parallel transfer. | Intrusive linked list of individually allocated objects is the antithesis of a device-resident SoA layout; the whole container must be rewritten before any of it runs on GPU. | XL | `src/lagrangian/basic/passiveParticle/passiveParticleCloud.H` |
| `patchInjectionBase` | custom-kernels | setPositionAndCell does THREE nested LINEAR searches (findArea over sumProcArea_, then sumFaceArea_, then sumFaceTriArea_[face]) to pick a processor, face and face-triangle by area fraction, then builds barycentric coordinates. | Linear search over per-face cumulative areas per injected parcel (O(nPatchFaces) each), plus Pstream::listCombineGather/Scatter of the per-processor area list. | `reactingCloud` | custom-kernels | Concrete instantiation of ReactingCloud; thermo table only. | Identical to ReactingCloud. | S | `src/lagrangian/parcel/clouds/derived/reactingCloud/reactingCloud.H` |
| `reactingMultiphaseCloud` | custom-kernels | Concrete instantiation of ReactingMultiphaseCloud for coal/biomass; thermo table only. | Identical to ReactingMultiphaseCloud. | S | `src/lagrangian/parcel/clouds/derived/reactingMultiphaseCloud/reactingMultiphaseCloud.H` |
| `solidParticle / solidParticleCloud` | custom-kernels | The physics is trivial (one-way drag with a Re-branched Cd plus buoyant gravity, a handful of flops per particle), but it is evaluated inside the base particle move/track loop with cell-value interpolation. | Inherits the full particle tracking blocker set; the drag closure itself would be a two-line device function. | L | `src/lagrangian/solidParticle/solidParticle.H` |

| `thermoCloud` | custom-kernels | Concrete instantiation of ThermoCloud; thermo selection table only. | Identical to ThermoCloud. | S | `src/lagrangian/parcel/clouds/derived/thermoCloud/thermoCloud.H` |

## finiteVolume discretisation schemes, surface interpolation and fvMatrix

### A-trivial  <sub>(251)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `CMULES` | custom-kernels | MULES::correct applies the same explicit limited flux correction on top of an already-solved bounded implicit field; identical face-loop structure to MULES::limiter. | Same scatter and dictionary blockers as MULES; the preceding implicit upwind solve is a separate fvMatrix (tier B) so this component pairs a B solve with an A correction. | L | `src/finiteVolume/fvMatrices/solvers/MULES/CMULES.H` |
| `CentredFitScheme<Type,Polynomial,Stencil>` | cuSPARSE | Runtime work is linear weights plus sum over the extended stencil of coeff_c*vf_c - a CSR SpMV from cells to faces. | extendedCellToFaceStencil::weightedSum builds List<List<Type>> stencilFld via collectData (a distributionMap gather) before summing; must be flattened to CSR and the MPI path dropped on a single GPU. | M | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/schemes/CentredFitScheme/CentredFitScheme.H` |
| `CoBlended<Type>` | custom-kernels | Face Courant number from \|phi\|*deltaT*deltaCoeffs/(magSf*rho) then a clamped linear ramp between Co1 and Co2 - all per-face arithmetic. | rho looked up by name for the compressible branch; deltaT is a host scalar pushed per step. | S | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/schemes/CoBlended` |
| `Function1s::laminarBL` | custom-kernels | f(yHat) = yHat*(2 - yHat) - two flops, evaluable in-kernel. | Currently invoked through the Function1 virtual interface on host; inline it as a __device__ function. | S | `src/finiteVolume/fields/fvPatchFields/derived/flowRateInletVelocity/laminarBL` |
| `Function1s::turbulentBL` | custom-kernels | f(yHat) = yHat^exponent (default 1/7) - a single pow, evaluable in-kernel. | Same Function1 virtual-dispatch indirection; inline as __device__. | S | `src/finiteVolume/fields/fvPatchFields/derived/flowRateInletVelocity/turbulentBL` |
| `GammaLimiter (Gamma / GammaV / limitedGamma / Gamma01)` | custom-kernels | limiter = min(max(phict/k,0),1) - three flops per face after the NVD helper. | None; k is a host scalar from the scheme text. | S | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/limitedSchemes/Gamma` |
| `LUST<Type>` | custom-kernels | Fixed 0.75/0.25 blend of linear and linearUpwind weights and corrections - two constant-weighted face kernels. | None. | S | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/schemes/LUST` |
| `Limited01Limiter<LimitedScheme>` | custom-kernels | LimitedLimiter hard-wired to [0,1]; the bounds become compile-time constants. | None. | S | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/limitedSchemes/Limited01/Limited01.H` |
| `LimitedLimiter<LimitedScheme>` | custom-kernels | Wraps a limiter and returns 0 when either cell value is outside [lower,upper] - two comparisons before the base limiter. | None; bounds are host scalars read at construction. | S | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/limitedSchemes/Limited/Limited.H` |
| `LimitedScheme<Type,Limiter,LimitFunc>` | custom-kernels | Read calcLimiter: LimitFunc maps the field to a scalar, fvc::grad gives cell gradients, then a flat face loop calls Limiter::limiter(CDweight, flux, phiP, phiN, gradP, gradN, d). Perfect one-thread-per-face kernel. | The Limiter is a compile-time policy class so it inlines as a __device__ functor; the only host touch is the fvSolution cache('limiter') test and objectRegistry store/lookupObjectRef of the cached limiter field, which happen per call. | M | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/limitedSchemes/LimitedScheme` |
| `MULES` | custom-kernels | Read MULESTemplates.C: explicitSolve is elementwise on cells; limiter() is face scatter-max/min into psiMaxn/psiMinn, face scatter-add into sumPhip/mSumPhim, then nLimiterIter (default 3) fixed passes of face->cell accumulate, per-cell clamp, face min-update. Bounded iteration count, no convergence test, no dynamic memory. | Heavy double-precision scatter-add and scatter-max/min (atomics or cell-face CSR); mesh.solution().solverDict(psi.name()) reads nLimiterIter/smoothLimiter/extremaCoeff from the dictionary on every call inside the time loop - hoist into a POD struct. Coupled-patch limiter min-reduction is MPI (no-op on one GPU). | L | `src/finiteVolume/fvMatrices/solvers/MULES` |
| `MUSCLLimiter (MUSCL / MUSCLV / limitedMUSCL / MUSCL01)` | custom-kernels | max(min(min(2r,0.5r+0.5),2),0) - min/max chain, branch-free. | None. | S | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/limitedSchemes/MUSCL` |
| `MinmodLimiter (Minmod / MinmodV)` | custom-kernels | max(min(r,1),0) - two instructions. | None. | S | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/limitedSchemes/Minmod` |
| `NVDTVD` | custom-kernels | Inline helpers computing gradf, gradcf, phict() and r() from scalars and vectors passed by value; no state. | None - annotate __host__ __device__ and it compiles as-is. | S | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/limitedSchemes/LimitedScheme/NVDTVD.H` |
| `NVDVTVDV` | custom-kernels | Vector form projecting tensor gradients onto the face vector difference; still pure inline arithmetic. | None; slightly higher register pressure from the tensor operands. | S | `src/finiteVolume/interpolation/surfaceInterpolation/limitedSchemes/LimitedScheme/NVDVTVDV.H` |
