| `meshSearch` | none (custom kernels); Thrust for the linear-argmin fallbacks | Holds two demand-driven octrees (indexedOctree<treeDataFace> boundary tree, indexedOctree<treeDataCell> cell tree) plus a walk-from-seed path. That is fine for the thousands-of-probes case and useless for a single probe. findNearestCellLinear is an O(nCells) argmin = one Thrust reduce. | mutable autoPtr octrees built lazily on first query - force construction and upload before the loop. findCell falls back to polyMesh::pointInCell tet tests, so the tet decomposition must be device-resident. | `regionSplit` | Thrust (sort/unique/lower_bound to replace the Map<label> compaction) | Connected components over the cell-face-cell graph: a local flood fill, an offset into a global numbering, a FaceCellWave<minData> merge across processor boundaries, and a final compaction. In practice this runs at construction, not per step, so the right answer for almost every solver is to leave it on the host. | Map<label> globalToCompact (regionSplit.C:187,289) and labelHashSet haveRegion (line 241) drive the compaction - replace with sort+unique+lower_bound. globalIndex plus Pstream::exchange (line 344) for the cross-rank merge (vanishes at one rank). List<labelHashSet> nonLocal (line 291). The FaceCellWave<minData> merge inherits that component's tier. | L (~1-2 mo) if genuinely needed on device; none if left on host | `src/meshTools/regionSplit/regionSplit.H` |

That changes the payload from unbounded to 8 bytes and makes the atomic update tractable. ## Mesh: Core mesh data structures

### A-trivial  <sub>(70)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `PatchTools::pointNormals / edgeNormals (PatchToolsNormals)` | none (plain CUDA) | Local arithmetic is an area-weighted sum of adjoining face normals followed by a normalise - a CSR gather per point/edge. | The 'parallel-consistent' part is a syncTools exchange between the sum and the normalise, which is MPI. On one GPU that step vanishes and the whole thing is two kernels. Cyclic patches still need the local duplicate-combine. | small (1-3 d) on a single GPU | `C:/Users/sdd32/Documents/GitHub/open_cfd/[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/meshes/primitiveMesh/PatchTools/PatchToolsNormals.C` |
| `PrimitivePatch normals and centres` | none (plain CUDA) | faceCentres/faceAreas/faceNormals are one thread per patch face over the local face CSR - same shape as the mesh-wide face geometry kernel. pointNormals is an area-weighted sum of adjoining face normals then a normalise. | pointNormal_p = normalise(sum over faces containing p of Sf) is a scatter-add over pointFaces - use the pointFaces CSR as a gather (deterministic) rather than atomics. Needed per step only on a moving mesh. | small (1-3 d) | `C:/Users/sdd32/Documents/GitHub/open_cfd/[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/meshes/primitiveMesh/PrimitivePatch/PrimitivePatchPointAddressing.C` |
| `PrimitivePatch::boundaryPoints` | CUB (DeviceSelect::Flagged) | Marks local points that touch a single-face (boundary) edge, then compacts. A flag-and-compact over the edge list. | Needs the edges/edgeFaces CSR to already exist on device, which for a static mesh means uploading it. Setup-only in practice. | trivial (<1 d) | `C:/Users/sdd32/Documents/GitHub/open_cfd/[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/meshes/primitiveMesh/PrimitivePatch/PrimitivePatchBdryPoints.C` |
| `Zone` | none (CUB for the inverse map) | A Zone is a labelList - a static index array that uploads directly and is read by fvModels/fvConstraints every step. Confirmed in Zone.C: the only heavy members are calcLookupMap() building a Map<label> (index -> zone-local position) and topoChange() which rebuilds via labelHashSet + sortedToc(). | Map<label> lookupMap must be replaced on device by either a full-size int8/int32 marker array over nCells (fastest, cheap at these sizes) or a sorted key array + binary search. topoChange()'s labelHashSet path is host-only and only fires on topology change. | small (1-3 d) | `C:/Users/sdd32/Documents/GitHub/open_cfd/[Foundation-12] src/OpenFOAM/meshes/zones/Zone/Zone.H` |

### B-sparse-solve  <sub>(3)</sub>

| Component | Library | Why | Blockers | Effort | Path |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `patchDistMethods::Poisson` | AMGX (or cuSPARSE + a hand CG) | Read PoissonPatchDistMethod.C: solve(fvm::laplacian(yPsi) == -1), then y = sqrt(magSqr(grad yPsi) + 2*yPsi) - mag(grad yPsi), and n = -grad/mag. One symmetric positive-definite Laplacian solve plus two elementwise field expressions and one gradient. This is the single cleanest GPU path for wall distance - a textbook AMG target. | Needs the fv Laplacian matrix assembled on device (or assembled on host once and handed to AMGX). Note it caches yPsi only when the mesh is changing and clears it otherwise - a small allocation churn to be aware of. Approximate distance (Spalding/Fares-Schroder/Tucker), so results differ from meshWave; verify turbulence-model sensitivity before switching. | small (1-3 d) once an AMGX solve path exists | `C:/Users/sdd32/Documents/GitHub/open_cfd/[Foundation-12] src/finiteVolume/fvMesh/wallDist/patchDistMethods/Poisson/PoissonPatchDistMethod.H` |
| `patchDistMethods::advectionDiffusion` | AMGX (asymmetric: BiCGStab/GMRES + AMG preconditioner) | Read the correct() loop: predicted by a simpler method (default Poisson), then iterates ny = grad(y) normalised, nf = normalised interpolate(ny), yPhi = Sf & nf, and solves div(yPhi,y) - Sp(div(yPhi),y) - epsilon*y*laplacian(y) == 1 until the initial residual falls below tolerance or maxIter (default 10). Every ingredient is a standard fv operator plus one asymmetric solve per iteration. | The matrix is ASYMMETRIC and its coefficients change every outer iteration (yPhi depends on grad y), so AMGX needs a coefficient update (and periodically a full setup) each pass - budget for that. The convergence check reads initialResidual on the host, which is a sync point per iteration; that is acceptable at ~10 iterations but must not be replicated in hot loops. | medium (1-2 wk) | `C:/Users/sdd32/Documents/GitHub/open_cfd/[Foundation-12] src/finiteVolume/fvMesh/wallDist/patchDistMethods/advectionDiffusion/advectionDiffusionPatchDistMethod.H` |
| `wallDist` | AMGX | A MeshObject holding y (and optionally n) and delegating to a runtime-selected patchDistMethod. For a STATIC mesh it is computed once at setup and thereafter is a read-only device field consumed by every turbulence model - the easy case. For a moving mesh it recomputes per motion step, and then the method choice decides everything: Poisson and advectionDiffusion are AMGX solves, meshWave is not portable. | The method is selected from fvSchemes, so a device build must steer users to Poisson (or advectionDiffusion) rather than the meshWave default. Being a MeshObject, its update policy determines whether y survives a mesh move or is deleted and rebuilt. | small (1-3 d) static; medium (1-2 wk) for the moving-mesh AMGX path | `C:/Users/sdd32/Documents/GitHub/open_cfd/[Foundation-12] src/finiteVolume/fvMesh/wallDist/wallDist/wallDist.H` |
